slug: big-mike-s-steakhouse-orange-beach
name: Big Mike's Steakhouse - Orange Beach
is_active: true
featured: false
entity_type: restaurant
entity_subtype: steak_house
description: Big Mike's Steakhouse is the vision of three friends, Mike Cole, Scott Powell, and Caine Conway. Big Mike is an experienced Chef and Restaurant Manager with ten years of steakhouse experience who had a vision for opening steakhouses in rural towns to provide a quality alternative for people who were tired of having to drive hours to get to the big city to enjoy a great steak. Scott and Caine shared in Mike's vision and brought their combined thirty plus years of business ownership experience to the team. Together the three friends began planning for their own steakhouse and when they found the location in Thomasville, Alabama they knew that it was time to start the business. Today you can find all three of them working in the restaurant most days. Mike manages the kitchen and is the creative force behind the menu and the food. Scott and Caine focus on the front of house and administrative functions of the restaurant and can be found greeting and talking with customers in the dining room and the bar. Today Big Mike's Steakhouse's success can be seen through its raving reviews, steady business during the week and long lines on Friday and Saturday. We are the place to go for a great steak, a special meal or a night out with friends. We have also had a number of our customers inquire about opening their own Big Mike's Steakhouse, which led us to launch our franchise program. Big Mike's has been more than these three friends ever expected, with enough lessons to fill a book. In fact that is what they are doing as part of the franchise program. All facets of the business have been documented from real estate to restaurant operations to human resources and beyond so that our franchisees can learn from our successes and our struggles. It is the assurance for our franchisees that they are in business for themselves but not by themselves. There is nothing like the taste of a wood-fire grilled steak, seasoned and cooked to perfection with the tenderness to be cut with a fork but deserving of the almighty steak knife. The smells and slightly smoky flavor stir great memories of cooking on the backyard grill with family and friends while enjoying this great American tradition of eating steak. A great steak has been called "one of the best things in the world" and those of us with a carnivorous spirit in us absolutely agree. For this lofty praise we are not talking about the mass produced beef served by the large chain steak restaurants who call themselves steakhouses. Instead a claim like this is usually reserved for steaks served by those big city expensive steakhouses where everything is sold a-la-carte and high prices make suitable for only a rare visit for very special occasions or on a company expense account. But now Big Mike's Steakhouse is proud to bring the "best thing in the world" steak eating experience to the people in small towns without the expense or drive that goes along with the big city steakhouses. Big Mike's is famous for our high quality steaks cooked to perfection on a wood-fired grill served in a relaxed rustic atmosphere in rural towns that have not previously been able to enjoy this type of true steakhouse experience. Big Mike's Steakhouse: steaks fitting for the big city, but served in your home towns.

--- entity_google_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
review_id: 7879
entity_slug: big-mike-s-steakhouse-orange-beach
google_review_name: places/ChIJa1zEB08GmogRqGZyF5wpKLc/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT201ak5ETXRYMHhyUlVoUVdqZzVTRWwzVkY4NGVHYxAB
rating: 2
review_text: As a general manager of a resort, I take reviews very seriously. So I hope this is able to give some insight so that Big Mike's can make some needed corrections.

I had been wanting to give this restaurant a try ever since moving to Orange Beach. Every time I drive by, the parking lot seems to always be at max capacity, so I had high expectations prior to arrival.

From a customer service standpoint, everything was great. Friendly staff greeted us at the door, and the waitress was very attentive.

I ordered the filet mignon medium-rare, with twice-baked potatoes and a Caesar salad as sides. Everything on my plate was great; it was one of the better steaks I've had in our travels around the United States over the past decade.

However, my wife's experience was not the same. Her steak was fairly tough, as was evident while I watched her try to cut it. Admittedly, it was cooked medium-well, but it still had a nice pink center. My wife mentioned that it wasn't very good, but she continued trying to eat it. It wasn't until I finished everything on my plate and she asked me to give hers a try that I was shocked by how terrible the taste was.

I'm not sure if the steak was left out too long or what transpired, but it had a terrible flavor reminiscent of meat that had not been kept at temperature or was beyond its freshness date.
Because my steak and meal were great, I want to give them the benefit of the doubt.

However, because I literally couldn't swallow my wife's steak, I have to rate this a two. Even if this was an anomaly, it is an indication of a lack of quality control in the kitchen, which is of concern.

--- entity_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
id: f8c48843-547b-41ce-9fbd-375c556dd752
entity_slug: big-mike-s-steakhouse-orange-beach
reviewer_name: Doran Eichenberg
rating: 2
body: As a general manager of a resort, I take reviews very seriously. So I hope this is able to give some insight so that Big Mike's can make some needed corrections.

I had been wanting to give this restaurant a try ever since moving to Orange Beach. Every time I drive by, the parking lot seems to always be at max capacity, so I had high expectations prior to arrival.

From a customer service standpoint, everything was great. Friendly staff greeted us at the door, and the waitress was very attentive.

I ordered the filet mignon medium-rare, with twice-baked potatoes and a Caesar salad as sides. Everything on my plate was great; it was one of the better steaks I've had in our travels around the United States over the past decade.

However, my wife's experience was not the same. Her steak was fairly tough, as was evident while I watched her try to cut it. Admittedly, it was cooked medium-well, but it still had a nice pink center. My wife mentioned that it wasn't very good, but she continued trying to eat it. It wasn't until I finished everything on my plate and she asked me to give hers a try that I was shocked by how terrible the taste was.

I'm not sure if the steak was left out too long or what transpired, but it had a terrible flavor reminiscent of meat that had not been kept at temperature or was beyond its freshness date.
Because my steak and meal were great, I want to give them the benefit of the doubt.

However, because I literally couldn't swallow my wife's steak, I have to rate this a two. Even if this was an anomaly, it is an indication of a lack of quality control in the kitchen, which is of concern.
verified_purchase: false
helpful_count: 0
approved: true
created_at: 2025-11-22T02:12:42.906185+00:00
updated_at: 2026-06-30T13:52:51.804126+00:00

subtitle: Big Mike's Steakhouse is the vision of three friends, Mike Cole, Scott Powell, and Caine Conway. Big Mike is an experienced Chef and Restaurant Manager with ten years of steakhouse experience who had a vision for opening steakhouses in rural towns to provide a quality alternative for people who were tired of having to drive hours to get to the big city to enjoy a great steak. Scott and Caine shared in Mike's vision and brought their combined thirty plus years of business ownership experience to the team. Together the three friends began planning for their own steakhouse and when they found the location in Thomasville, Alabama they knew that it was time to start the business. Today you can find all three of them working in the restaurant most days. Mike manages the kitchen and is the creative force behind the menu and the food. Scott and Caine focus on the front of house and administrative functions of the restaurant and can be found greeting and talking with customers in the dining room and the bar. Today Big Mike's Steakhouse's success can be seen through its raving reviews, steady business during the week and long lines on Friday and Saturday. We are the place to go for a great steak, a special meal or a night out with friends. We have also had a number of our customers inquire about opening their own Big Mike's Steakhouse, which led us to launch our franchise program. Big Mike's has been more than these three friends ever expected, with enough lessons to fill a book. In fact that is what they are doing as part of the franchise program. All facets of the business have been documented from real estate to restaurant operations to human resources and beyond so that our franchisees can learn from our successes and our struggles. It is the assurance for our franchisees that they are in business for themselves but not by themselves. There is nothing like the taste of a wood-fire grilled steak, seasoned and cooked to perfection with the tenderness to be cut with a fork but deserving of the almighty steak knife. The smells and slightly smoky flavor stir great memories of cooking on the backyard grill with family and friends while enjoying this great American tradition of eating steak. A great steak has been called "one of the best things in the world" and those of us with a carnivorous spirit in us absolutely agree. For this lofty praise we are not talking about the mass produced beef served by the large chain steak restaurants who call themselves steakhouses. Instead a claim like this is usually reserved for steaks served by those big city expensive steakhouses where everything is sold a-la-carte and high prices make suitable for only a rare visit for very special occasions or on a company expense account. But now Big Mike's Steakhouse is proud to bring the "best thing in the world" steak eating experience to the people in small towns without the expense or drive that goes along with the big city steakhouses. Big Mike's is famous for our high quality steaks cooked to perfection on a wood-fired grill served in a relaxed rustic atmosphere in rural towns that have not previously been able to enjoy this type of true steakhouse experience. Big Mike's Steakhouse: steaks fitting for the big city, but served in your home towns.
